Because the wrapper body cannot contain conditionals, all orchestration lives in the skill — the wrapper just shells out. - **cookie**: `cookie` writes one browser cookie by assigning a string to `document.cookie`. It is the declarative counterpart of `document.cookie = "name=value;max-age=…;path=…"`: the attributes can be bound to sources with tokens, and `triggers` makes the command write the cookie again whenever a source changes. Use it to persist a small value such as a selected language, a view mode or a timestamp across pag ([Manzoomeh/BasisCore.Client-v2](https://github.com/Manzoomeh/BasisCore.Client-v2/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/cookie.md) · ★5)
